const state=()=>({at:new Date().toISOString(),p:bot.entity.position.clone(),hp:bot.health,food:bot.food,dim:bot.game.dimension,ground:bot.entity.onGround,inv:bot.inventory.items().map(i=>[i.name,i.count,i.durabilityUsed])});

const dirs=[v(0,-1,0),v(0,1,0),v(0,0,-1),v(0,0,1),v(-1,0,0),v(1,0,0)];
async function face(q){
 const eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),block=bot.blockAt(q),points=[q.offset(.5,.5,.5)];let obstruction=null;
 for(const sh of block?.shapes||[])for(const axis of[0,1,2])for(const end of[0,1])for(const u of[.25,.5,.75])for(const w of[.25,.5,.75]){const co=[0,0,0],others=[0,1,2].filter(x=>x!==axis);co[axis]=sh[axis+3*end]+(end?-.001:.001);co[others[0]]=sh[others[0]]+(sh[others[0]+3]-sh[others[0]])*u;co[others[1]]=sh[others[1]]+(sh[others[1]+3]-sh[others[1]])*w;points.push(q.offset(...co));}
 for(const aim of points){const d=aim.minus(eye),dist=d.norm();if(dist>4.4||dist<.001)continue;const h=bot.world.raycast(eye,d.scaled(1/dist),dist+.02);if(!h?.position.equals(q)){if(h)obstruction={p:h.position,n:bot.blockAt(h.position)?.name};continue;}await bot.lookAt(aim,true);await wait(1);const c=bot.blockAtCursor(4.5);if(c?.position.equals(q))return c;}
 throw Error('no visible reachable face '+q+' obstruction '+JSON.stringify(obstruction));
}
async function cut(q){const b=bot.blockAt(q);if(['air','cave_air'].includes(b?.name))return;if(['water','lava','obsidian','chest','crafting_table','nether_portal'].includes(b?.name)||b?.name?.endsWith('_bed'))throw Error('protected '+b?.name);const tool=bot.inventory.items().find(i=>(['stone','granite','diorite','andesite','cobblestone','deepslate'].includes(b.name)||b.name.endsWith('_ore'))?i.name.endsWith('_pickaxe'):['dirt','grass_block','snow','sand'].includes(b.name)?i.name.endsWith('_shovel'):b.name.endsWith('_log')?i.name.endsWith('_axe'):false);if(tool)await bot.equip(tool,'hand');else await bot.unequip('hand');const f=await face(q);await bot.dig(b,'ignore',dirs[f.face]);await wait(2);if(!['air','cave_air'].includes(bot.blockAt(q)?.name))throw Error('digpost');console.log('CUT',JSON.stringify({q,n:b.name,p:bot.entity.position}));}
async function up(){const p=bot.entity.position.floored(),q=p.offset(0,-1,0);if(!bot.entity.onGround||!full(q))throw Error('UPsource');await cut(p.offset(0,2,0));const item=['dirt','gravel','cobblestone','spruce_planks'].map(n=>bot.inventory.items().find(i=>i.name===n)).find(Boolean);if(!item)throw Error('UPfund');await bot.equip(item,'hand');await bot.lookAt(q.offset(.5,1,.5),true);bot.setControlState('jump',true);try{for(let k=0;k<12;k++){await wait(1);if(bot.entity.position.y>=p.y+.8){const c=bot.blockAtCursor(4.5);if(!c?.position.equals(q))throw Error('UPcursor');await bot.placeBlock(bot.blockAt(q),v(0,1,0));break;}}}finally{bot.setControlState('jump',false);}await wait(8);if(!bot.entity.onGround||Math.abs(bot.entity.position.y-(p.y+1))>.05)throw Error('UPpost');console.log('UP',JSON.stringify(state()));}
function stop(){bot.pathfinder.setGoal(null);bot.clearControlStates();}
async function walk(x,y,z,ms=12000,minHP=1){const m=new Movements(bot);m.canDig=false;m.allow1by1towers=false;m.allowParkour=false;m.allowSprinting=false;m.maxDropDown=2;m.scafoldingBlocks=[];for(const n of['water','lava','powder_snow'])if(bot.registry.blocksByName[n])m.blocksToAvoid.add(bot.registry.blocksByName[n].id);bot.pathfinder.setMovements(m);const g=new goals.GoalBlock(x,y,z);bot.pathfinder.setGoal(g);const t=Date.now();try{while(Date.now()-t<ms&&!g.isEnd(bot.entity.position.floored())){if(bot.health<minHP)throw Error('walkhealth');await wait(2);}}finally{stop();}await wait(3);if(!g.isEnd(bot.entity.position.floored())||!bot.entity.onGround)throw Error('walkpost '+JSON.stringify(state()));console.log('WALK',JSON.stringify(state()));}
async function tidy(){if(bot.currentWindow)bot.closeWindow(bot.currentWindow);for(let k=1;k<=4;k++)if(bot.inventory.slots[k])await bot.putAway(k);await wait(2);if(bot.inventory.selectedItem){const e=Array.from({length:36},(_,i)=>i+9).find(i=>!bot.inventory.slots[i]);if(e===undefined)throw Error('cursorfull');await bot.clickWindow(e,0,0);await wait(2);}}

async function up(){const p=bot.entity.position.floored(),q=p.offset(0,-1,0);if(!bot.entity.onGround||!full(q))throw Error('UPsource');await cut(p.offset(0,2,0));const item=['dirt','gravel','cobblestone','spruce_planks'].map(n=>bot.inventory.items().find(i=>i.name===n)).find(Boolean);if(!item)throw Error('UPfund');await bot.equip(item,'hand');await bot.lookAt(q.offset(.5,1,.5),true);bot.setControlState('jump',true);try{for(let k=0;k<12;k++){await wait(1);if(bot.entity.position.y>=p.y+.8){const c=bot.blockAtCursor(4.5);if(!c?.position.equals(q))throw Error('UPcursor');await bot.placeBlock(bot.blockAt(q),v(0,1,0));break;}}}finally{bot.setControlState('jump',false);}await wait(8);if(!bot.entity.onGround||Math.abs(bot.entity.position.y-(p.y+1))>.05)throw Error('UPpost');console.log('UP',JSON.stringify(state()));}

async function placeAt(q,n){if(!['air','cave_air'].includes(bot.blockAt(q)?.name))throw Error('placeoccupied '+q);const item=bot.inventory.items().find(i=>i.name===n);if(!item)throw Error('placefund '+n);for(const d of dirs){const ref=q.minus(d);if(!full(ref))continue;const aim=ref.offset(.5+d.x*.49,.5+d.y*.49,.5+d.z*.49),eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),delta=aim.minus(eye);if(delta.norm()>4.4)continue;const h=bot.world.raycast(eye,delta.scaled(1/delta.norm()),delta.norm()+.04);if(!h?.position.equals(ref))continue;await bot.lookAt(aim,true);await wait(1);const c=bot.blockAtCursor(4.5);if(!c?.position.equals(ref)||!dirs[c.face]?.equals(d))continue;await bot.equip(item,'hand');await bot.placeBlock(bot.blockAt(ref),d);await wait(2);if(bot.blockAt(q)?.name!==n)throw Error('placepost');return;}throw Error('placeface '+q);}

async function makeBox(hint=null,open=false){

const state=()=>({at:new Date().toISOString(),p:bot.entity.position.clone(),hp:bot.health,food:bot.food,dim:bot.game.dimension,ground:bot.entity.onGround,inv:bot.inventory.items().map(i=>[i.name,i.count,i.durabilityUsed])});

const dirs=[v(0,-1,0),v(0,1,0),v(0,0,-1),v(0,0,1),v(-1,0,0),v(1,0,0)];
async function face(q){
 const eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),block=bot.blockAt(q),points=[q.offset(.5,.5,.5)];let obstruction=null;
 for(const sh of block?.shapes||[])for(const axis of[0,1,2])for(const end of[0,1])for(const u of[.25,.5,.75])for(const w of[.25,.5,.75]){const co=[0,0,0],others=[0,1,2].filter(x=>x!==axis);co[axis]=sh[axis+3*end]+(end?-.001:.001);co[others[0]]=sh[others[0]]+(sh[others[0]+3]-sh[others[0]])*u;co[others[1]]=sh[others[1]]+(sh[others[1]+3]-sh[others[1]])*w;points.push(q.offset(...co));}

 throw Error('no visible reachable face '+q+' obstruction '+JSON.stringify(obstruction));
}
async function cut(q){const b=bot.blockAt(q);if(['air','cave_air'].includes(b?.name))return;if(['water','lava','obsidian','chest','crafting_table','nether_portal'].includes(b?.name)||b?.name?.endsWith('_bed'))throw Error('protected '+b?.name);if(b.name==='snow'&&!b.shapes.length){const eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),aim=q.offset(.5,.1,.5),d=aim.minus(eye),h=bot.world.raycast(eye,d.scaled(1/d.norm()),d.norm()+.01);if(d.norm()>4.4||(h&&!h.position.equals(q)))throw Error('snow view');await bot.lookAt(aim,true);await wait(1);await bot.dig(b,true,'raycast');await wait(2);return;}const tool=bot.inventory.items().find(i=>(['stone','granite','diorite','andesite','cobblestone','deepslate'].includes(b.name)||b.name.endsWith('_ore'))?i.name.endsWith('_pickaxe'):['dirt','grass_block','snow','sand'].includes(b.name)?i.name.endsWith('_shovel'):b.name.endsWith('_log')?i.name.endsWith('_axe'):false);if(tool)await bot.equip(tool,'hand');else await bot.unequip('hand');const f=await face(q);await bot.dig(b,'ignore',dirs[f.face]);await wait(2);if(!['air','cave_air'].includes(bot.blockAt(q)?.name))throw Error('digpost');console.log('CUT',JSON.stringify({q,n:b.name,p:bot.entity.position}));}

const state=()=>({at:new Date().toISOString(),p:bot.entity.position.clone(),hp:bot.health,food:bot.food,dim:bot.game.dimension,ground:bot.entity.onGround,inv:bot.inventory.items().map(i=>[i.name,i.count,i.durabilityUsed])});

const dirs=[v(0,-1,0),v(0,1,0),v(0,0,-1),v(0,0,1),v(-1,0,0),v(1,0,0)];
async function face(q){
 const eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),block=bot.blockAt(q),points=[q.offset(.5,.5,.5)];let obstruction=null;
 for(const sh of block?.shapes||[])for(const axis of[0,1,2])for(const end of[0,1])for(const u of[.01,.25,.5,.75,.99])for(const w of[.01,.25,.5,.75,.99]){const co=[0,0,0],others=[0,1,2].filter(x=>x!==axis);co[axis]=sh[axis+3*end]+(end?-.001:.001);co[others[0]]=sh[others[0]]+(sh[others[0]+3]-sh[others[0]])*u;co[others[1]]=sh[others[1]]+(sh[others[1]+3]-sh[others[1]])*w;points.push(q.offset(...co));}
 for(const aim of points){const d=aim.minus(eye),dist=d.norm();if(dist>4.4||dist<.001)continue;const h=bot.world.raycast(eye,d.scaled(1/dist),dist+.02);if(!h?.position.equals(q)){if(h)obstruction={p:h.position,n:bot.blockAt(h.position)?.name};continue;}await bot.lookAt(aim,true);await wait(1);const c=bot.blockAtCursor(4.5);if(c?.position.equals(q))return c;}
 throw Error('no visible reachable face '+q+' obstruction '+JSON.stringify(obstruction));
}
async function cut(q){const b=bot.blockAt(q);if(['air','cave_air'].includes(b?.name))return;if(['water','lava','obsidian','chest','crafting_table','nether_portal'].includes(b?.name)||b?.name?.endsWith('_bed'))throw Error('protected '+b?.name);if(b.name==='snow'&&!b.shapes.length){const eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),aim=q.offset(.5,.1,.5),d=aim.minus(eye),h=bot.world.raycast(eye,d.scaled(1/d.norm()),d.norm()+.01);if(d.norm()>4.4||(h&&!h.position.equals(q)))throw Error('snow view');await bot.lookAt(aim,true);await wait(1);await bot.dig(b,true,'raycast');await wait(2);return;}const tool=bot.inventory.items().find(i=>(['stone','granite','diorite','andesite','cobblestone','deepslate'].includes(b.name)||b.name.endsWith('_ore'))?i.name.endsWith('_pickaxe'):['dirt','grass_block','snow','sand'].includes(b.name)?i.name.endsWith('_shovel'):b.name.endsWith('_log')?i.name.endsWith('_axe'):false);if(tool)await bot.equip(tool,'hand');else await bot.unequip('hand');const f=await face(q);await bot.dig(b,'ignore',dirs[f.face]);await wait(2);if(!['air','cave_air'].includes(bot.blockAt(q)?.name))throw Error('digpost');console.log('CUT',JSON.stringify({q,n:b.name,p:bot.entity.position}));}
async function up(){const p=bot.entity.position.floored(),q=p.offset(0,-1,0);if(!bot.entity.onGround||!full(q))throw Error('UPsource');await cut(p.offset(0,2,0));const item=['dirt','gravel','cobblestone','spruce_planks'].map(n=>bot.inventory.items().find(i=>i.name===n)).find(Boolean);if(!item)throw Error('UPfund');await bot.equip(item,'hand');await bot.lookAt(q.offset(.5,1,.5),true);bot.setControlState('jump',true);try{for(let k=0;k<12;k++){await wait(1);if(bot.entity.position.y>=p.y+.8){const c=bot.blockAtCursor(4.5);if(!c?.position.equals(q))throw Error('UPcursor');await bot.placeBlock(bot.blockAt(q),v(0,1,0));break;}}}finally{bot.setControlState('jump',false);}await wait(8);if(!bot.entity.onGround||Math.abs(bot.entity.position.y-(p.y+1))>.05)throw Error('UPpost');console.log('UP',JSON.stringify(state()));}

async function walk(x,y,z,ms=12000,minHP=1){const m=new Movements(bot);m.canDig=false;m.allow1by1towers=false;m.allowParkour=false;m.allowSprinting=false;m.maxDropDown=2;m.scafoldingBlocks=[];for(const n of['water','lava','powder_snow'])if(bot.registry.blocksByName[n])m.blocksToAvoid.add(bot.registry.blocksByName[n].id);bot.pathfinder.setMovements(m);const g=new goals.GoalBlock(x,y,z);bot.pathfinder.setGoal(g);const t=Date.now();try{while(Date.now()-t<ms&&!g.isEnd(bot.entity.position.floored())){if(bot.health<minHP)throw Error('walkhealth');await wait(2);}}finally{stop();}await wait(3);if(!g.isEnd(bot.entity.position.floored())||!bot.entity.onGround)throw Error('walkpost '+JSON.stringify(state()));console.log('WALK',JSON.stringify(state()));}

async function craft(n,k=1,table=null){await tidy();const r=bot.recipesFor(bot.registry.itemsByName[n].id,null,1,table)[0];if(!r)throw Error('recipe '+n);if(table)await face(table.position);await bot.craft(r,k,table);await wait(3);await tidy();console.log('CRAFT',n,k,JSON.stringify(state()));}
async function placeAt(q,n){if(!['air','cave_air'].includes(bot.blockAt(q)?.name))throw Error('placeoccupied '+q);const item=bot.inventory.items().find(i=>i.name===n);if(!item)throw Error('placefund '+n);for(const d of dirs){const ref=q.minus(d);if(!full(ref))continue;const aim=ref.offset(.5+d.x*.49,.5+d.y*.49,.5+d.z*.49),eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),delta=aim.minus(eye);if(delta.norm()>4.4)continue;const h=bot.world.raycast(eye,delta.scaled(1/delta.norm()),delta.norm()+.04);if(!h?.position.equals(ref))continue;await bot.lookAt(aim,true);await wait(1);const c=bot.blockAtCursor(4.5);if(!c?.position.equals(ref)||!dirs[c.face]?.equals(d))continue;await bot.equip(item,'hand');await bot.placeBlock(bot.blockAt(ref),d);await wait(2);if(bot.blockAt(q)?.name!==n)throw Error('placepost');return;}throw Error('placeface '+q);}
async function fightNearby(ms=6500){const t=Date.now();let last=0;while(Date.now()-t<ms&&bot.health>0){const p=bot.entity.position;const e=Object.values(bot.entities).filter(e=>['zombie','spider','skeleton'].includes(e.name)&&e.position.distanceTo(p)<5).sort((a,b)=>a.position.distanceTo(p)-b.position.distanceTo(p))[0];if(!e)break;const weapon=bot.inventory.items().find(i=>i.name==='stone_sword')||bot.inventory.items().find(i=>i.name==='wooden_sword');if(!weapon)throw Error('no sword for fight');await bot.equip(weapon,'hand');const eye=p.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),aim=e.position.offset(0,Math.min(1,e.height*.7),0),d=aim.minus(eye);if(e.position.distanceTo(p)<=3&&d.norm()<=3&&!bot.world.raycast(eye,d.scaled(1/d.norm()),d.norm()-.05)){await bot.lookAt(aim,true);if(Date.now()-last>600){bot.attack(e);last=Date.now();console.log('ORDINARY_HIT',JSON.stringify({at:new Date().toISOString(),n:e.name,id:e.id,p,ep:e.position,hp:bot.health}));}}await wait(2);}console.log('FIGHT_END',JSON.stringify(state()));}

stop();console.log('SOURCE',JSON.stringify(state()));const p=bot.entity.position.floored();console.log('LOCAL',JSON.stringify(Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i-4).flatMap(x=>Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i-4).flatMap(z=>Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=>i-1).map(y=>{const q=p.offset(x,y,z),b=bot.blockAt(q);return {q,n:b?.name,f:full(q)};})))));console.log('ENTITIES',JSON.stringify(Object.values(bot.entities).filter(e=>e.position.distanceTo(bot.entity.position)<24).map(e=>({id:e.id,n:e.name,p:e.position,meta:e.metadata}))));return state();

async function face(q){

}

async function face(q){

 for(const sh of block?.shapes||[])for(const axis of[0,1,2])for(const end of[0,1])for(const u of[.01,.25,.5,.75,.99])for(const w of[.01,.25,.5,.75,.99]){const co=[0,0,0],others=[0,1,2].filter(x=>x!==axis);co[axis]=sh[axis+3*end]+(end?-.001:.001);co[others[0]]=sh[others[0]]+(sh[others[0]+3]-sh[others[0]])*u;co[others[1]]=sh[others[1]]+(sh[others[1]+3]-sh[others[1]])*w;points.push(q.offset(...co));}

}
async function cut(q){const b=bot.blockAt(q);if(['air','cave_air'].includes(b?.name))return;if(['water','lava','obsidian','chest','crafting_table','nether_portal'].includes(b?.name)||b?.name?.endsWith('_bed'))throw Error('protected '+b?.name);if(b.name==='snow'&&!b.shapes.length){const eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),aim=q.offset(.5,.1,.5),d=aim.minus(eye),h=bot.world.raycast(eye,d.scaled(1/d.norm()),d.norm()+.01);if(d.norm()>4.4||(h&&!h.position.equals(q)))throw Error('snow view');await bot.lookAt(aim,true);await wait(1);await bot.dig(b,true,'raycast');await wait(2);return;}const tool=bot.inventory.items().find(i=>(['stone','granite','diorite','andesite','cobblestone','deepslate'].includes(b.name)||b.name.endsWith('_ore'))?i.name.endsWith('_pickaxe'):['dirt','grass_block','snow','sand'].includes(b.name)?i.name.endsWith('_shovel'):b.name.endsWith('_log')?i.name.endsWith('_axe'):false);if(tool)await bot.equip(tool,'hand');else await bot.unequip('hand');const f=await face(q);await bot.dig(b,'ignore',dirs[f.face]);await wait(2);if(!['air','cave_air'].includes(bot.blockAt(q)?.name))throw Error('digpost');console.log('CUT',JSON.stringify({q,n:b.name,p:bot.entity.position}));}

async function face(q){
 const eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),block=bot.blockAt(q),points=[q.offset(.5,.5,.5)];let obstruction=null;
 for(const sh of block?.shapes||[])for(const axis of[0,1,2])for(const end of[0,1])for(const u of[.01,.25,.5,.75,.99])for(const w of[.01,.25,.5,.75,.99]){const co=[0,0,0],others=[0,1,2].filter(x=>x!==axis);co[axis]=sh[axis+3*end]+(end?-.001:.001);co[others[0]]=sh[others[0]]+(sh[others[0]+3]-sh[others[0]])*u;co[others[1]]=sh[others[1]]+(sh[others[1]+3]-sh[others[1]])*w;points.push(q.offset(...co));}

}
async function cut(q){const b=bot.blockAt(q);if(['air','cave_air'].includes(b?.name))return;if(['water','lava','obsidian','chest','crafting_table','nether_portal'].includes(b?.name)||b?.name?.endsWith('_bed'))throw Error('protected '+b?.name);if(b.name==='snow'&&!b.shapes.length){const eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),aim=q.offset(.5,.1,.5),d=aim.minus(eye),h=bot.world.raycast(eye,d.scaled(1/d.norm()),d.norm()+.01);if(d.norm()>4.4||(h&&!h.position.equals(q)))throw Error('snow view');await bot.lookAt(aim,true);await wait(1);await bot.dig(b,true,'raycast');await wait(2);return;}const tool=bot.inventory.items().find(i=>(['stone','granite','diorite','andesite','cobblestone','deepslate'].includes(b.name)||b.name.endsWith('_ore'))?i.name.endsWith('_pickaxe'):['dirt','grass_block','snow','sand'].includes(b.name)?i.name.endsWith('_shovel'):b.name.endsWith('_log')?i.name.endsWith('_axe'):false);if(tool)await bot.equip(tool,'hand');else await bot.unequip('hand');const f=await face(q);await bot.dig(b,'ignore',dirs[f.face]);await wait(2);if(!['air','cave_air'].includes(bot.blockAt(q)?.name))throw Error('digpost');console.log('CUT',JSON.stringify({q,n:b.name,p:bot.entity.position}));}

async function face(q){

 for(const sh of block?.shapes||[])for(const axis of[0,1,2])for(const end of[0,1])for(const u of[.01,.25,.5,.75,.99])for(const w of[.01,.25,.5,.75,.99]){const co=[0,0,0],others=[0,1,2].filter(x=>x!==axis);co[axis]=sh[axis+3*end]+(end?-.001:.001);co[others[0]]=sh[others[0]]+(sh[others[0]+3]-sh[others[0]])*u;co[others[1]]=sh[others[1]]+(sh[others[1]+3]-sh[others[1]])*w;points.push(q.offset(...co));}

async function cut(q){const b=bot.blockAt(q);if(['air','cave_air'].includes(b?.name))return;if(['water','lava','obsidian','chest','crafting_table','nether_portal'].includes(b?.name)||b?.name?.endsWith('_bed'))throw Error('protected '+b?.name);if(b.name==='snow'&&!b.shapes.length){const eye=bot.entity.position.offset(0,bot.entity.eyeHeight,0),aim=q.offset(.5,.1,.5),d=aim.minus(eye),h=bot.world.raycast(eye,d.scaled(1/d.norm()),d.norm()+.01);if(d.norm()>4.4||(h&&!h.position.equals(q)))throw Error('snow view');await bot.lookAt(aim,true);await wait(1);await bot.dig(b,true,'raycast');await wait(2);return;}const tool=bot.inventory.items().find(i=>(['stone','granite','diorite','andesite','cobblestone','deepslate'].includes(b.name)||b.name.endsWith('_ore'))?i.name.endsWith('_pickaxe'):['dirt','grass_block','snow','sand'].includes(b.name)?i.name.endsWith('_shovel'):b.name.endsWith('_log')?i.name.endsWith('_axe'):false);if(tool)await bot.equip(tool,'hand');else await bot.unequip('hand');const f=await face(q);await bot.dig(b,'ignore',dirs[f.face]);await wait(2);if(!['air','cave_air'].includes(bot.blockAt(q)?.name))throw Error('digpost');console.log('CUT',JSON.stringify({q,n:b.name,p:bot.entity.position}));}
